Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion has recently emerged as a promising direction for high-fidelity image generation by modeling images directly in the original pixel domain. However, pixel-space diffusion is computationally demanding due to the extremely high dimensionality of natural images. For efficiency, existing pixel diffusion models either compromise fine details with large-patch tokenization or confine subsequent refinement within individual patches. Such intra-patch refinement inevitably restricts structural continuity across patch boundaries and long-range token interactions, limiting refinement quality. To address these issues, we propose PixSGR, a novel Pixel diffusion framework with Sparse Global Refinement tailored for modeling the distribution of natural images directly in pixel space. PixSGR starts from a supervised low-channel bottleneck to efficiently capture the low-dimensional manifold of natural images. It then progressively expands the channel dimensionality and spatial resolution to recover increasingly fine-grained structures. At the spatial refinement stage, coarse-scale attention maps preselect globally relevant interactions to pre-sparsify fine-scale attention, enabling non-local refinement beyond isolated patches without the quadratic cost of dense attention. Extensive experiments on ImageNet validate the effectiveness of PixSGR. It achieves an FID of 1.51 at 256$\times$256 and maintains performance when scaled to 512$\times$512, attaining an FID of 1.60.

</details>


### [45] [ReBridge-Flow: Re-Coupling Posterior Bridges in Flow Matching for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00811)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Yiqi Wang,Hongjie Wu,Bohan Guo,Xinan Wang,Zichen Luo,Taotao Cai,Zhi Chen,Mingkai Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBridge-Flow方法，通过后验桥重新耦合解决流匹配图像恢复中测量约束导致的端点对不兼容问题，提升恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过局部校正来融入测量约束，这种校正可能破坏预训练流隐式编码的源-干净端点耦合，使得校正后的端点对与当前状态不兼容，导致桥接不匹配问题。

Method: 提出ReBridge-Flow方法：1)给定当前状态，解码对应的局部源端和干净端点；2)通过干净端锚定融入测量信息，同步重新耦合源端点，产生具有改进局部桥接兼容性的测量感知端点对；3)重新耦合的端点定义后验知情传输方向以推进采样过程。同时引入后验桥缺陷(Posterior Bridge Defect)来联合表征测量误差、与流先验的偏差和桥接不匹配，并导出干净端锚定和源端重新耦合的显式更新。

Result: 在多个自然和医学图像恢复任务上的广泛实验表明，ReBridge-Flow有效缓解了桥接不匹配问题，提高了恢复图像的结构一致性。

Conclusion: ReBridge-Flow通过后验桥重新耦合方法，有效缓解了桥接不匹配问题，提高了恢复图像的结构一致性，在自然图像和医学图像 restoration 任务上都取得了良好效果。

Abstract: Flow Matching provides an efficient generative prior for image restoration by learning continuous transport between source and data distributions. However, existing methods typically incorporate measurement constraints through local corrections. Such corrections may disrupt the source-clean endpoint coupling implicitly encoded by the pretrained flow, making the corrected endpoint pair incompatible with the current state. To address this issue, we propose ReBridge-Flow, a posterior bridge re-coupling method. Specifically, given the current state, ReBridge-Flow first decodes the corresponding local source and clean endpoints. It then incorporates measurement information through clean-side anchoring and synchronously re-couples the source endpoint, yielding a measurement-aware endpoint pair with improved local bridge compatibility. The re-coupled endpoints further define a posterior-informed transport direction for advancing the sampling process. We also introduce the Posterior Bridge Defect, which jointly characterizes measurement error, deviation from the flow prior, and bridge mismatch, and leads to explicit updates for clean-side anchoring and source-side re-coupling. Extensive experiments on multiple natural and medical image restoration tasks demonstrate that ReBridge-Flow effectively alleviates bridge mismatch and improves the structural consistency of restored images.

</details>


### [46] [RingMoClaw: An Experience-Inspired Multi-Agent Framework for Self-Evolving Research in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00814)
*Kaiyue Kang,Qixuan He,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Chao Ren,Kangxin Wang,Wenhui Diao,Yixiao Wang,Liangjin Zhao,Kaiwen Wei,Nayu Liu,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RingMoClaw是一个受经验启发的自进化多智能体框架，用于遥感视觉解释。该框架通过研究分支、质量控制分支和双流动态经验总线的集成，建立了覆盖策略生成、实验执行、独立审查和经验积累的闭环优化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感视觉模型持续推动各种解释任务的进步，但模型改进的研究过程仍严重依赖手动专业知识，需要在模型设计、数据处理和性能诊断方面进行大量试错迭代。现有基于智能体的方法主要关注任务执行和工作流编排，缺乏持续性能优化的自主研究迭代能力。

Method: RingMoClaw集成了研究分支、质量控制分支和双流动态经验总线，建立了闭环优化过程。它采用异构Critic机制提供阶段性诊断和反馈，双流经验总线整合外部知识和内部实验经验来指导策略演变并消除无效搜索。

Result: 在四个遥感下游任务（目标检测、场景分类、语义分割和变化检测）上的广泛实验表明，RingMoClaw有效且可泛化。相比基线模型，RingMoClaw在目标检测上提高了1.84% mAP50，并在其他三个任务上获得一致增益，同时与现有研究自动化框架相比减少了超过40%的进化步骤。

Conclusion: RingMoClaw为从任务执行到持续研究驱动的遥感模型演变提供了一条可行的路径。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual models have continuously advanced various interpretation tasks. However, the research process behind model improvement still heavily relies on manual expertise, requiring extensive trial-and-error iterations in model design, data processing, and performance diagnosis. Existing agent-based approaches mainly focus on task execution and workflow orchestration, while lacking the capability of autonomous research iteration for continuous performance optimization. To address this issue, we propose RingMoClaw, an experience-inspired self-evolving multi-agent framework for remote sensing visual interpretation. RingMoClaw integrates a research branch, a quality-control branch, and a dual-stream dynamic experience bus to establish a closed-loop optimization process covering strategy generation, experiment execution, independent review, and experience accumulation. The heterogeneous Critic mechanism provides stage-wise diagnosis and feedback, while the dual-stream experience bus incorporates external knowledge and internal experimental experience to guide strategy evolution and eliminate ineffective searches. Extensive experiments on four remote sensing downstream tasks, including object detection, scene classification, semantic segmentation, and change detection, demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of RingMoClaw. Compared with the corresponding baseline models, RingMoClaw improves performance by 1.84\% mAP$_{{50}}$ on object detection and achieves consistent gains across the other three tasks, while reducing the required evolution steps by over 40\% compared with existing research automation frameworks. These results suggest that RingMoClaw offers a feasible route from task execution toward continuous research driven model evolution in remote sensing.

</details>


### [47] [Can Scene Text Recognition Read Rare Compositions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00816)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 场景文本识别在标准基准上准确率达89-97%，但当按词频和字符n-gram新颖性分层测试时，稀有词×稀有三字词的准确率比中心区域下降10-18个百分点，且缩放模型规模无法改善这一现象，暗示需要架构改变而非增加模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准显示高准确率，但作者认为这掩盖了稀有词识别能力的严重不足。需要通过词频和字符n-gram新颖性对测试集进行联合分层，揭示模型在长尾分布上的真实表现。

Method: 1)构建5×5网格按词频和三字词新颖性分层测试；2)测试9个英语专业识别器和13个跨4种文字系统的模型对；3)进行视觉骨干网络6倍缩放实验；4)使用四种探针方法定位失败原因：层探针、错误时置信度、注意力重平衡、跨脚本提交vs放弃错误分解；5)测试16种非架构缓解方法；6)测试SVTRv2的AR-to-CTC架构转换和AR-CTC集成。

Result: 稀有词×稀有三字词角落准确率比Q3/Q3中心下降10-18个百分点，且在所有13个语言-模型对上方向一致；视觉骨干放大(CLIP4STR-Base 158M→Huge 1.0B)使整体基准提升但压力角落无改善(86.9→86.5)；失败定位于自回归解码器的词汇先验；16种非架构缓解最大增益仅+1.3pt；SVTRv2的CTC解码实现+2.5pt(p=0.02)且唯一超越配对Bootstrap噪声；置信度路由AR-CTC集成增益+0.6pt但仍在噪声内；无配置同时改善组合角落和整体准确率。

Conclusion: 稀有输入的长尾问题指向架构变革而非容量增加。自回归解码器的词汇先验是性能瓶颈，CTC解码架构是唯一有效改善的干预措施。

Abstract: Scene text recognition is reported as 89--97% accurate on the six standard benchmarks, and the problem is widely treated as saturated. We present an alternative reading. When the same test images are stratified jointly by ground-truth word rarity and character n-gram novelty against a reference corpus, accuracy at the rare-word x rare-trigram corner of the resulting 5x5 grid drops 10--18 pt below the q3/q3 centre across nine English specialised recognisers, and the same direction (corner below centre) holds on all 13 of 13 (language, model) pairs we test across four writing systems (Latin, Han, Han+kana, Arabic). The drop is not a capacity bottleneck. A 6x vision-backbone scale-up (CLIP4STR-Base 158M -> CLIP4STR-Huge 1.0B, OpenCLIP ViT-H/14 LAION-2B) leads every benchmark in aggregate accuracy yet leaves the stress corner unchanged (86.9 -> 86.5, within paired-bootstrap noise). Four converging probes--layer-wise probing, confidence-when-wrong, attention re-balancing, and a cross-script commit-vs-abstain error split--localise the failure to the autoregressive decoder's lexical prior. We then ask how much of the gap existing techniques recover. Of 16 non-architectural mitigations, the largest mean q5/q5 gain is +1.3 pt and none clears the paired-bootstrap noise floor; the only intervention that does is the architectural shift from autoregressive to CTC decoding (SVTRv2, +2.5 pt, p=0.02, n=474). A confidence-routed AR-CTC ensemble adds a directionally consistent +0.6 pt that stays within noise, and its dominant learned coefficient is each model's own minimum-softmax confidence--independently echoing the mechanism above. No configuration we test improves both the compositional corner and aggregate accuracy. The rare-input long tail thus points to architectural change rather than added capacity.

</details>


### [48] [Visual Attention Faithfulness in Vision-Language Models is Heterogeneous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00830)
*Xurui Song,Weishi Wang,Zhongqi Yue,Kuluhan Binici,Tao Bai,Hongxin Shao,Daniel Dahlmeier,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过因果扰动分析研究视觉-语言模型(VLM)中注意力权重是否能忠实反映模型推理，发现视觉注意力忠实性呈现三种不同处理模式：忠实-充分、忠实-分布和非聚焦，且与人类标注区域存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP中注意力权重是否忠实反映模型推理的问题已引发广泛讨论，但视觉模态中这一问题尚未被充分探索。本文旨在通过因果扰动分析填补这一研究空白。

Method: 对当前VLM进行因果扰动分析，评估注意力排序视觉标记的全面性和充分性差距。在VQAv2、VRDU和ChartQA数据集上验证三种处理模式。

Result: 发现三种处理模式：1)忠实-充分：top-k注意力标记对预测必要且充分；2)忠实-分布：必要但需要更广泛视觉上下文；3)非聚焦：无局部注意力区域单独必要但视觉信息仍是预测触发因素。人类标注区域仅在约60%情况下满足全面性，揭示了模型视觉依赖与人类直觉的系统性分歧。

Conclusion: 视觉注意力忠实性随处理需求和模型架构系统变化，而非统一忠实或不忠实。这一发现表明VLM的注意力机制与人类视觉注意存在本质差异，需要重新审视注意力权重作为模型推理解释工具的有效性。

Abstract: Whether attention weights faithfully reflect model reasoning has been actively debated in NLP, yet this question remains largely unexplored for the visual modality in Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We address this gap through causal perturbation analysis on current VLMs, evaluating both the comprehensiveness and sufficiency gap of attention-ranked visual tokens. Our analysis reveals that visual attention faithfulness is heterogeneous, manifesting in three distinct processing modes: Faithful-Sufficient, where top-$k$ attention tokens are both necessary and sufficient for prediction; Faithful-Distributed, where they are necessary but broader visual context remains required; and Non-Focal, where no localized attention region is individually necessary while visual information remains an essential trigger for prediction. Furthermore, human-annotated ground-truth regions satisfy comprehensiveness in only $\sim 60$% of cases compared with model attention rankings, revealing systematic divergence between model visual reliance and human intuition. We demonstrate these patterns across both general VQA on VQAv2 and document tasks on VRDU and ChartQA, showing that visual attention faithfulness varies systematically with processing demands and model architectures rather than being uniformly faithful or unfaithful.

</details>


### [49] [Residual Kalman Dynamics for Event-Based UAV Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00839)
*Per Nyblom,Hannes Ovrén,David Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究事件相机数据集FRED上的无人机边界框预测，提出了基于常速度卡尔曼滤波器的残差模型，通过结合历史框信息、滤波状态特征和局部事件表示来预测加速度修正量，在短中期预测任务上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决事件相机数据集中无人机边界框的短期和中期预测问题。事件相机相比传统相机具有高动态范围和低延迟优势，但现有方法可能未能充分利用其优势。需要一个强物理基线来评估深度学习方法的真正提升。

Method: 1)使用常速度卡尔曼滤波器作为物理基线，处理完整的中心-尺寸框状态；2)训练残差模型预测加速度类修正值，输入包括：近期边界框历史、滤波状态特征和局部事件表示；3)引入解耦子集作为诊断压力测试，分析位置-速度捷径对结果的影响。

Result: 1)残差模型始终优于卡尔曼基线；2)事件条件化模型表现最佳；3)残差目标部分可从锚点位置和速度预测，表明FRED结果同时反映视觉证据和数据集特定的运动先验；4)事件条件化残差模型在位置-速度捷径被削弱时仍保留有用的预测信号。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单而有效的残差预测框架，事件信息对于提升预测性能至关重要。同时揭示了FRED数据集中运动先验的存在，为公平评估视觉预测方法提供了改进思路。

Abstract: We study short- and mid-horizon UAV bounding-box forecasting on the FRED event-camera dataset. We use a constant-velocity Kalman filter over a full center-size box state as a strong physical baseline, and train a residual model to predict acceleration-like corrections from recent box history, filtered state features, and local event representations. This simple residual formulation consistently improves over the Kalman baseline, with event-conditioned models giving the strongest results among the evaluated methods. We further show that part of the residual target is predictable from anchor position and velocity alone, indicating that canonical FRED results can reflect both visual evidence and dataset-specific motion priors. To analyze this effect, we introduce decorrelated subsets as a diagnostic stress test, showing that event-conditioned residual models retain useful predictive signal even when measured position- and velocity-based shortcuts are weakened.

</details>


### [50] [An Intelligent Decision Support System for Emotion Monitoring using Microscopic Fixational Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00846)
*Xiangyu Shen,Feiyang Deng,Zijian Dai,Aibin Chen,Jizheng Yi,Jie Li,Hongbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于智能眼镜和智能手机的边缘计算情感监测系统，通过分析微眼动（微扫视、眼漂移、微震颤）推断情绪状态，结合多头注意力机制、XGBoost和SVM实现83.6%的个性化F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前情绪监测方法依赖面部或生理信号，存在侵入性高和隐私问题；传统宏观眼动指标区分度有限，需要更精细的微眼动特征来实现无创、连续的情绪推断。

Method: 1) 提取三类微眼动特征：微扫视、眼漂移、眼震颤；2) 构建混合AI管道：多头注意力机制提取时序特征，XGBoost量化特征重要性，SVM进行设备端分类；3) 采用留一被试交叉验证；4) 引入少样本个性化机制适应个体差异。

Result: 60名志愿者实验表明，微眼动特征比传统宏观特征更具区分性；个性化F1分数达83.6%，在受控和自然移动场景下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了可解释、无侵入、可部署的实时情绪监测范式，为连续心理状态追踪提供了新的生理学基础和技术路径。

Abstract: The rising prevalence of psychological disorders necessitates effective emotion monitoring, yet current methods relying on facial or physiological signals often suffer from intrusiveness and privacy issues. This paper proposes an intelligent decision support system and pervasive edge-computing framework that leverages smart glasses and a companion smartphone to infer emotional states from microscopic visual fixation patterns. Moving beyond traditional macroscopic gaze metrics, the proposed system extracts and decomposes three distinct neurophysiological micro-movements: microsaccades, ocular drifts, and ocular microtremors. We introduce an interpretable hybrid artificial intelligence pipeline combining a multi-head attention mechanism, extreme gradient boosting, and a support vector machine to extract deep temporal features, quantify their physiological importance, and perform efficient on-device classification. Through an extensive evaluation involving 60 volunteers, we rigorously validate the framework under a strict leave-one-subject-out cross-validation protocol across both controlled and naturalistic mobile scenarios. Ablation studies unequivocally demonstrate that these fixational micro-movements are substantially more discriminative for emotion inference than traditional macroscopic features. Furthermore, aligned with contemporary affective science, the system incorporates a few-shot personalization mechanism to bridge universal physiological baselines with individual emotional heterogeneity, achieving a highly robust personalized F1-score of 83.6%. This work establishes a physiologically interpretable, unobtrusive, and deployable paradigm for continuous real-time emotion monitoring.

</details>


### [51] [ADGNet: Asymmetric Dual-text Guided Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00853)
*Tongtong Wang,Mingzhu Xu,Chenglong Yu,Jing Wang,Xiaohui Lin,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非对称双文本引导网络(ADGNet)，用于解决红外小目标检测(IRSTD)中的难题。该方法针对当前多模态方法中单一文本提示无法区分目标和背景的问题，设计了非对称双文本提示(ADP)和非对称双分支交互(ADBI)模块，分别用文本先验引导视觉特征，保护目标免受噪声干扰同时抑制背景杂波。实验表明该方法优于21种最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测是一项具有挑战性的任务。纯视觉方法仅依赖像素级信息，难以区分目标和杂波。当前多模态方法通常使用单一文本提示描述目标和背景，缺乏针对性的区域引导，忽略了红外语义不对称性。这导致背景抑制信息不足，且引入严重的特征优化冲突，使小目标被噪声淹没。

Method: 提出非对称双文本引导网络(ADGNet)：(1)设计非对称双文本提示(ADP)，包含图像无关的抽象目标提示和图像相关的详细背景提示，以处理红外语义不对称；(2)引入非对称双分支交互(ADBI)模块，分别用文本先验引导视觉特征，保护目标并抑制背景；(3)引入自适应特征聚合(AFA)模块动态融合双分支特征；(4)构建多模态非对称图像-文本红外(AITIR)数据集，对三个公共数据集进行非对称文本标注。

Result: 大量实验表明，ADGNet在AITIR数据集上优于21种最先进方法(SOTA)。

Conclusion: 提出的非对称双文本引导策略有效解决了红外小目标检测中背景抑制不足和特征优化冲突的问题，通过分离目标与背景的文本引导，显著提升了小目标检测性能。

Abstract: InfRared Small Target Detection (IRSTD) is a challenging task. Relying solely on pixel-level information, vision-only methods struggle to distinguish targets from clutter. Current multimodal methods typically describe both targets and backgrounds with a single textual prompt. Such an approach lacks dedicated regional guidance and ignores infrared semantic asymmetry. Consequently, it provides insufficient background suppression information and introduces severe feature optimization conflicts, overwhelming small targets with noise. To address these issues, we propose a novel Asymmetric Dual-text Guided Network (ADGNet). Specifically, accounting for the infrared semantic asymmetry, we first design the Asymmetric Dual-text Prompt (ADP), comprising an image-agnostic abstract target prompt and an image-specific detailed background prompt. To leverage these prompts, we introduce an Asymmetric Dual-Branch Interaction (ADBI) module to separately guide visual features with their respective text priors, protecting targets from noise while suppressing background clutter. Subsequently, we introduce an Adaptive Feature Aggregation (AFA) module to dynamically fuse features from the two branches. Furthermore, we construct a multimodal Asymmetric Image-Text Infrared (AITIR) dataset by providing asymmetric text annotations for three public datasets (IRSTD-1K, NUDT-SIRST, and SIRST). Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADGNet outperforms 21 state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. Code is available at https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/MM26-ADGNet.

</details>


### [52] [Training-Free Inpainting Across Domains with a Frozen Text-to-Image Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00862)
*Zhenhuan Wang,Fengyi Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Step-PI方法，无需针对inpainting的权重训练或数据集特定适配，仅使用固定控制器配置即可在多个自然图像域实现条件inpainting。该方法通过已知区域投影结合边界-内部潜在反馈、持久PI状态和预定义的四场释放调度来调制反向轨迹上的控制器信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将冻结的通用文本到图像扩散模型用于inpainting通常需要大量针对inpainting的离线优化，缺乏一种无需训练即可跨域应用的通用方法。

Method: Step-PI方法包含三个核心组件：1)已知区域投影；2)边界-内部潜在反馈；3)持久PI状态；4)预定义的四场释放调度。在CelebA-HQ上开发，使用固定控制器配置，无需任何inpainting特定权重训练或数据集适配即可迁移到AFHQ和Places2。

Result: 在3500个案例的两项对比中，添加持久状态和替换均匀释放为预定义调度均显著提升所有15个数据集-指标单元；95%bootstrap置信区间均排除零。在与LanPaint和PILOT（基于vanilla SD1.5的最佳无训练基线）的比较中，Step-PI在所有五个等数据集宏指标上领先。

Conclusion: Step-PI为将冻结的通用文本到图像模型重新用于跨域inpainting提供了一种互补方法，通过测试时潜在控制实现无需训练的跨域迁移。

Abstract: We show that a frozen generic text-to-image diffusion model can perform conditional inpainting across three evaluated natural-image domains with one fixed controller configuration, without inpainting-specific weight training, dataset-specific weight adaptation, or learned inpainting-specific conditioning channels. Step-PI augments known-region projection with boundary-interior latent feedback, persistent PI state, and a predefined four-field release schedule that modulates controller signals along the reverse trajectory. Developed only on Main35-disjoint CelebA-HQ pilots, the controller transfers unchanged to AFHQ and Places2. Across two field-identical comparisons on the same 3,500 cases, adding persistent state and replacing uniform release with the predefined schedule each improve all 15 dataset-metric cells; 95% bootstrap intervals exclude zero for all five metrics in both comparisons. In descriptive native-route comparisons, Step-PI leads LanPaint and PILOT (the closest evaluated training-free baselines using vanilla SD1.5) on all five equal-dataset macro metrics. Inpainting-trained systems retain the absolute metric leads but rely on substantial inpainting-specific offline optimization. Our method provides a complementary approach for repurposing a frozen generic text-to-image model for cross-domain inpainting through test-time latent control.

</details>


### [53] [Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Automated Pathology Diagnosis and Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00866)
*Yumi Lee,Harim Oh,Hyoryung Kim,Minji Kim,Eunsu Kim,Hyeseong Lee,Junya Fukuoka,Andrey Bychkov,Jijgee Munkhdelger,Rajiv Kumar Kaushal,Ayushi Sahay,Rajni Yadav,Bharathi Prabakaran,Sulen Sarioglu,Serdar Balcı,Ilknur Turkmen,Yuri Tolkach,Christian Harder,Julian Westerdorf,Reinhard Buettner,Audun Ljone Henriksen,Sepp De Raedt,Byung Hyun Lee,Sungjin Lim,Joohoon Lee,Gwanghyun Kim,Se Young Chun,Suryakant Singh,Saarthak Kapse,Prateek Prasanna,Kyung A Kim,Yousun Kang,Sehwan Yoo,Sungman Hong,Shubham Innani,Michael Feldman,Spyridon Bakas,Ujjwal Baid,Prasad Dutande,Suhas Gajare,Bhakti Baheti,Serkan Sökmen,Ece Tuğba Cebeci,Ahmet Halıcı,Musa Balcı,Kardelen Peçenek,Srividhya Sainath,Kyongseok Jang,Messi H. J. Lee,Noorul Wahab,Bodong Du,Jiaming Zhang,Qixiang Zhang,Jang-Hwan Choi,Sangjeong Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REG 2025是一个用于评估全切片图像(WSI)结构化报告生成的基准测试，基于约10,500对来自5个机构的Pan-Asia WSI-报告数据集。顶级方法并非仅依赖VLM，而是通过结构化报告表示、分层诊断分解和多模态接地实现更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决全切片图像(WSI)病理报告生成中的两大挑战：1) 大规模WSI-报告配对数据集的稀缺；2. 将空间分布的视觉模式映射到结构化临床文本的复杂性

Method: 1) 构建了临床精选的Pan-Asia WSI-报告数据集，包含约10,500对样本，来自5个机构；2) 通过MICCAI挑战建立REG 2025基准进行系统评估；3) 分析了多种方法：预训练VLM、多示例学习框架、分层专家模型、检索增强生成、跨模态Transformer

Result: VLM单独使用不足以获得最佳性能；表现最好的方法受益于结构化报告表示、分层诊断分解和有效的多模态接地。关键局限性包括：定量属性估计不稳定(如数值幻觉)和过度诊断倾向，部分错误类似于常规诊断中的已知陷阱

Conclusion: REG 2025作为评估WSI结构化报告生成和计算病理学中视觉语言理解的基准，为设计临床可行的多模态病理模型提供见解

Abstract: The rapid advancement of vision-language models (VLMs) has accelerated progress in computational pathology; however, whole-slide image (WSI)-based pathology report generation remains limited by the scarcity of large-scale WSI--report datasets and the complexity of mapping spatially distributed visual patterns to structured clinical text. To address this, we introduce a clinically curated Pan-Asia WSI--report dataset of approximately 10,500 pairs from five institutions and establish the REG 2025 benchmark through a MICCAI challenge for systematic evaluation of multimodal models. We analyze submitted methods spanning pretrained VLMs, multiple-instance learning frameworks, hierarchical expert models, retrieval-augmented generation, and cross-modal Transformers. Rather than indicating that VLM use alone was sufficient for superior performance, the results suggest that top-performing methods benefited from structured report representations, hierarchical diagnostic decomposition, and effective multimodal grounding. We identify key limitations, including instability in quantitative attribute estimation (e.g., numeric hallucination) and a tendency toward diagnostic overspecification, with some errors resembling known diagnostic pitfalls in routine pathology. These findings establish REG 2025 as a benchmark for evaluating WSI-based structured report generation and vision-language understanding in computational pathology, providing insights for the design of clinically grounded multimodal pathology models.

</details>


### [54] [The Visual Insensitivity Gap: Diagnosing When Vision-Language Models Fail to Use Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00868)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究者发现视觉-语言模型(VLM)在40-97%的样本中实际上忽略了视觉输入，仅依赖文本进行预测，这一现象被称为"视觉不敏感gap"，并提出VSI指标来量化这个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态基准测试假设VLM会使用视觉输入，但这一假设从未被明确验证。研究动机是揭示模型是否真正利用了视觉信息。

Method: 通过模糊问题相关的视觉区域，观察next-token分布是否发生变化；使用线性探针检测视觉编码器能否区分干净/扰动图像；跨6个VLM和3个感知基准进行实验。

Result: 40-97%样本存在视觉不敏感；VSI在模型间相关(rho=0.40, p<10^-3)；视觉编码器准确率72-79%但LLM仅2-11%改变预测，存在>0.65的编码器-LLM差距；多选推理AUROC=0.85-0.87。

Conclusion: 视觉不敏感是样本固有属性，VSI是有效的诊断工具，适用于多选推理场景但不适合事实性任务，最佳用途是作为条件集成组件。

Abstract: Vision-language models are evaluated by aggregate accuracy on multimodal benchmarks, a practice that implicitly assumes the model uses its visual input. We show this assumption fails on 40%--97% of samples across six VLMs and three perceptual benchmarks: blurring the question-relevant visual region leaves the next-token distribution nearly unchanged. We name this phenomenon the Visual Insensitivity Gap and quantify it with a per-sample Visual Sensitivity Index (VSI). The gap is a property of samples, not of models: VSI ranks correlate across models (grand-mean Spearman rho=+0.40, permutation p<10^-3), so the same samples are flagged insensitive by VLMs sharing no architectural detail beyond a contrastively pretrained vision tower. The mechanism is concrete: on the insensitive samples, a linear probe on each model's own vision tower distinguishes perturbed from clean images at 0.72--0.79 accuracy, yet the model's argmax token changes on only 2%--11% of the same samples, an encoder--LLM gap above 0.65 on every model. Mapping VSI's diagnostic utility cell by cell surfaces a strong regime (multi-choice reasoning on capable VLMs: AUROC=0.85--0.87) and a weak regime (well-calibrated factuality, where softmax confidence already leads). VSI is not a universal best abstention signal; it is a sample-intrinsic indicator of vision-ignoring failure, best used as a conditional ensemble component.

</details>


### [55] [Vision-Language-Guided Pseudo-Labels for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation in Semantic Segmentation for Waste Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00898)
*Udo Schlegel,Shubhangi,Gabriel Dax,Sai Rahul Kaminwar,Florian Karl,Thomas Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种跨模态伪标签管道，用于无监督域自适应语义分割，利用SAM生成类无关区域提议，EVA-CLIP基于区域-文本相似性分配语义标签，并通过可选的BLIP进行语言验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶、工业废物分拣等应用场景中，获取语义分割的标注数据成本高昂且难以大规模获取，需要无需目标域标注的无监督域自适应方法。

Method: 核心管道：SAM生成类无关区域提议，EVA-CLIP基于区域-文本相似度分配语义标签，置信度过滤确保仅使用可靠伪标签进行自训练；可选的BLIP为模糊区域提供语言接地验证。

Result: 在合成到真实的自动驾驶域迁移和实验室到工厂的工业废物分拣两个域迁移上，一致性地优于仅使用源域的基线模型。

Conclusion: 伪标签质量而非数量是域迁移下自训练的决定性因素，跨模态语言接地为部署关键应用提供了可靠的自动标注实用路径。

Abstract: Obtaining labeled data for semantic segmentation in applied settings (e.g., autonomous driving, industrial waste sorting) is expensive and often infeasible at scale. We present a cross-modal pseudo-labeling pipeline that enables unsupervised domain adaptation without any target-domain annotations. The pipeline is built on two core foundation models: SAM generates class-agnostic region proposals, and EVA-CLIP assigns semantic labels based on region-text similarity, with confidence filtering ensuring that only reliable pseudo-labels are used for self-training a segmentation model. As an optional extension, BLIP provides language-grounded verification for ambiguous regions, thereby improving pseudo-label quality without altering the overall pipeline. Evaluated on two domain shifts, synthetic-to-real autonomous driving and, with a primary focus, lab-to-factory industrial waste sorting, the pipeline consistently improves over source-only baselines. Our results demonstrate that pseudo-label quality, not quantity, is a decisive factor in self-training under domain shift, and that cross-modal language grounding offers a practical path to reliable automatic annotation in deployment-critical applications.

</details>


### [56] [HELIOS: From midnight to noon, continuous outdoor urban scene relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00901)
*Hala Djeghim,Nathan Piasco,Luis Roldão,Moussab Bennehar,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Céline Loscos,Désiré Sidibé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HELIOS是一种用于自动驾驶场景的新型图像重光照方法，通过结合基于反射率的调节和循环一致扩散管道，在无配对真实世界数据的情况下实现日夜照明转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖合成数据或配对的多光照监督，导致在真实世界多样化条件下的泛化能力受限。自动驾驶场景需要处理从白天到夜晚的各种光照条件，但缺乏大规模配对数据。

Method: 1) 提出基于反射率的调节机制，集成到循环一致扩散管道中防止身份崩塌；2) 引入鲁棒的反射率蒸馏策略，从白天域迁移结构稳定性以处理低能见度的夜间条件；3) 使用GPS衍生的太阳角度替代传统文本提示，实现精细控制。

Result: 在夜到日和日到夜任务中生成结构一致、真实的结果，在定性和定量评估以及用户研究中均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HELIOS能够仅使用无标签真实世界数据进行训练，成功实现日夜间的平滑连续光照操控，为自动驾驶场景的图像重光照提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modifying the illumination of driving images is a fundamental challenge, as most datasets are captured at specific times of day. Existing methods rely on synthetic data or paired multi-illumination supervision, which limits their generalization to the diverse and challenging conditions of real-world scenarios. To address this, we propose HELIOS, a novel image relighting approach that relies on unlabeled real-world datasets without requiring any paired images for training. Our approach integrates albedo-based conditioning into a cycle-consistent diffusion pipeline to prevent identity collapse and ensure accurate domain translation. To handle low-visibility nighttime conditions, we introduce a robust albedo distillation strategy that transfers structural stability from the daytime domain. Additionally, we replace traditional text prompts with a fine-grained control mechanism based on GPS-derived solar angles, enabling smooth and continuous lighting manipulation across the day-night cycle. Through extensive evaluation and a user study, we demonstrate that HELIOS produces structurally consistent and realistic results in both night-to-day and day-to-night tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [57] [A multicenter benchmark and clinically structured metric for coronary CTA report generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00909)
*Zhiyu Ye,Yue Sun,Limiao Zou,Cheng Xu,Keting Xu,Tong Hu,Yue Yu,Hairong Zheng,Yining Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究团队建立了首个四中心CCTA报告生成基准数据集（3021个序列，818对患者报告），并开发了临床结构化评估指标CSM_CCTA，用于评估开放源代码的3D视觉语言模型在CCTA报告生成任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化CCTA报告生成的可靠评估缺乏标准化多中心基准和临床结构化指标，现有指标无法充分反映解剖学特异性和临床发现一致性。

Method: 1) 构建四中心基准数据集，包含3021个CCTA序列和818对患者-报告对；2) 开发CSM_CCTA指标，包含患者级、血管级和节段级变量，参考临床指南定义；3) 基于70个专家评分案例估计权重，并在30个非重叠案例中评估临床对齐；4) 评估7个开源3D视觉语言模型。

Result: CSM_CCTA与放射科医生评分高度相关（Pearson r=0.97, p<0.001），比次优指标FORTE（r=0.70）高出0.27；与专家偏好在160个成对比较中一致115次（71.9%）；对临床等效措辞稳定，随信息 omission单调下降。C2RG模型在所有四家医院获得最高CSM_CCTA分数，但仍远未达最优；通用模型高达98.7%输出与CCTA无关。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了标准化的模型比较设置，CSM_CCTA实现了临床对齐且解剖学分辨率的报告评估。该方法支持更临床对齐、解剖学解析的CCTA报告生成评估方法。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of automated coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA) report generation requires standardized multicentre benchmarks and clinically structured metrics. We established a four-centre benchmark comprising 3,021 CCTA series from 818 patient-report pairs to evaluate seven open-source three-dimensional vision-language models. We developed CSM$_{{\text{{CCTA}}}}$, a clinically structured metric for CCTA report evaluation, with patient-, vessel-, and segment-level variables defined according to clinical guidelines. Report pairs are compared at the finest shared anatomical level, and the contributions of different clinical components are weighted based on expert assessments. We estimated these weights using 70 expert-scored cases and evaluated clinical alignment in a non-overlapping set of 30 cases. CSM$_{{\text{{CCTA}}}}$ showed a strong correlation with radiologist scores (Pearson's $r=0.97$, $p<0.001$), exceeding the next-best metric, FORTE ($r=0.70$), by 0.27, and agreed with expert preferences in 115 of 160 pairwise comparisons (71.9\%). Under controlled perturbations, CSM$_{{\text{{CCTA}}}}$ remained stable to clinically equivalent wording and decreased monotonically with progressive information omission. In the multicenter benchmark, the CCTA-trained C2RG model achieved the highest CSM$_{{\text{{CCTA}}}}$ scores across all four hospitals, although its performance remained far from optimal. In contrast, CCTA-irrelevant reports accounted for up to 98.7\% of the outputs from generalist models. Together, the benchmark provides a standardized setting for model comparison, while CSM$_{{\text{{CCTA}}}}$ enables clinically structured evaluation of finding agreement and anatomical specificity. These results support a more clinically aligned and anatomically resolved approach to evaluating CCTA report generation. Code is available at https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenMedIA/CSM_CCTA.

</details>


### [58] [On-the-Fly3R: Towards Robust Online 3D Reconstruction with Feed-Forward 3R Models for Large-Scale UAV Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00923)
*Zhe Shen,Liyuan Lou,Yifei Yu,Guanbo Wang,Quanjian Ji,Xin Wang,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: On-the-Fly3R是一个无需训练的大型无人机图像渐进式在线3D重建框架，可将各种3R模型扩展到超过5000张图像的平方公里级无人机场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建(3R)虽能高效端到端建模，但Transformer注意力导致内存成本过高，难以应用于大规模无人机测绘；现有可扩展流式3R方法假设时空连续输入，对跨带无人机操作中常见的弱序或无序图像流无效。

Method: 1. 通过检索引导的动态子集构造实现无序输入重建，自适应选择空间相关图像；2. 设计验证-拒绝-重试机制进行预集成一致性检查，自动拒绝错位图像并用替代子集重试以保证全局一致性；3. 借鉴VSLAM，基于检索闭环进行姿态图优化以减少相机漂移。

Result: 在多个无人机基准数据集上，On-the-Fly3R成功将各种3R模型扩展到超过5000张图像的平方公里级无人机场景，精度显著优于多种SOTA流式3R方法。

Conclusion: On-the-Fly3R是一个无需训练的渐进式在线3D重建框架，能够将各种3R backbone升级以适应大规模无人机场景，为大规模无人机测绘提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While feed-forward 3D reconstruction (3R) offers efficient end-to-end modeling, its application in large-scale UAV mapping is hindered by the prohibitive memory cost of Transformer attention. Current scalable streaming 3R methods assume temporally and spatially continuous inputs, rendering them ineffective for the weakly ordered or unordered image streams common in cross-strip UAV operations. To address this, we propose On-the-Fly3R, a training-free, progressive online 3D reconstruction framework for large-scale UAV images that upgrades various 3R backbones for large-scale UAV scenarios. Our method enables reconstruction from unordered inputs via retrieval-guided dynamic subset construction, which adaptively selects spatially relevant images. To further improve the robustness, a validation-rejection-retry mechanism is designed to guarantee global consistency, performing a pre-integration consistency check and automatically rejecting misaligned images and retrying with alternative subset. Finally, inspired by VSLAM, pose graph optimization based on the retrieval loop closure is employed to mitigate camera drift. Evaluations on several UAV benchmarks show that our On-the-Fly3R successfully scales various 3R models to over 5,000 images across square-kilometer UAV scenes, delivering substantially superior accuracy compared to several SOTA streaming 3R methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Sh1nZzz/On_the_Fly3R

</details>


### [59] [Beyond the Image Plane: World-Grounded Queries for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00924)
*Orcun Cetintas,Guillem Brasó,Tim Meinhardt,Laura Leal-Taixé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLANET是一个端到端的多目标跟踪器，通过将2D图像平面跟踪提升到3D世界坐标来解决传统跟踪器的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频将3D场景记录为2D图像平面投影，隐藏了深度和空间关系。现有多目标跟踪器主要使用图像平面上的外观和几何信息进行定位和关联，继承了这些歧义问题。

Method: 1）将现有2D跟踪数据集提升到3D；2）通过将重建的3D场景几何嵌入到特征和位置编码中形成世界锚定查询；3）使用辅助3D位置预测任务鼓励查询编码对象位置；4）使用双分辨率时间记忆保留跨长时间间隔的证据。

Result: 在三个不同的基准数据集上实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: PLANET成功超越了图像平面的限制，通过3D场景几何实现了更准确的多目标跟踪。

Abstract: Monocular videos record 3D scenes as sequences of 2D image-plane projections, obscuring depth and spatial relationships. Multi-object trackers localize and associate objects primarily using appearance and geometry observed only in the image plane, inheriting these ambiguities. To address this limitation, we introduce PLANET, an end-to-end multi-object tracker designed to move beyond the image plane. As an enabling step, we lift existing 2D tracking datasets into 3D. We then form world-grounded queries by embedding reconstructed 3D scene geometry into the features and positional encodings used during query formation. An auxiliary 3D location prediction task further encourages the queries to encode object positions during training. A complementary dual-resolution temporal memory preserves this evidence across longer temporal gaps. As a result, PLANET achieves state-of-the-art performance across three diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [60] [From Terminology to Diagrams: Visual-Instruction Generation for Scientific Diagram Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00948)
*Raul Ortega,José Manuel Gómez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciGram框架，通过基于科学课程术语的大规模图表指令数据生成方法，构建了包含194K图表和140万条视觉指令的数据集，显著提升了视觉语言模型在科学图表问答上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在自然图像问答上表现良好，但在科学图表理解上存在困难。科学图表传达的是功能或关系意义，而非 literal scenes（字面场景），现有模型难以处理这种特殊类型的图像。

Method: 提出一个基于术语的图表指令数据生成框架：(1)从科学课程中提取领域概念；(2)合成原子事实；(3)从网络检索相关图表；(4)生成多模态监督信号（图表描述和多项选择题）。

Result: 在SciGram上微调的模型在TQA、ScienceQA和AI2D等图表中心基准测试中取得显著提升，超越或匹配当前最优的VLM，且使用更少的训练实例。增强LLaVA OneVision后在图表问答上达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 术语驱动的指令生成是提升科学领域视觉语言推理的有效通用策略。发布了SciGram数据集和模型以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in visual question answering with natural images. However, they continue to struggle with scientific diagrams, which are designed to convey functional or relational meaning rather than literal scenes. We therefore introduce a framework for generating large-scale diagram-grounded instruction data by leveraging terminology derived from scientific curricula. Our approach systematically extracts domain concepts, synthesizes atomic facts, retrieves relevant diagrams from the web, and generates multimodal supervision in the form of diagram captions and multiple-choice questions. Using this pipeline, we construct SciGram, a dataset of over 194K diagrams and 1.4M visual instructions across life, earth, and physical sciences. Despite relying on noisy web data and synthetic annotations, models fine-tuned on SciGram achieve substantial improvements on diagram-centric benchmarks, including TQA, ScienceQA, and AI2D, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art VLMs while using fewer training instances. Furthermore, augmenting existing models such as LLaVA OneVision with SciGram establishes new state-of-the-art performance on diagram question answering. Our results highlight the effectiveness of terminology-grounded instruction generation as a general strategy for improving vision-language reasoning in scientific domains. To support future research in scientific diagram understanding, we release both the SciGram dataset and models.

</details>


### [61] [CERF: Communication-Efficient and Retraining-Free Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00951)
*Jiuwu Hao,Ziyi Ni,Liguo Sun,Yuting Wan,Yueyang Wu,Ti Xiang,Haolin Song,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CERF框架，解决协作感知中通信开销大和对异构代理支持不足的问题。通过引入虚拟模态Poture和时序预测技术，实现高效低成本的跨代理感知协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作感知方法依赖传输和融合密集特征图，造成高通信开销和异构性挑战，难以在实际场景中部署。需要一种通信高效且能支持开放异构多智能体的协作感知方案。

Method: 1) 提出虚拟模态Poture，从其他智能体的感知输出生成，用于增强ego智能体的BEV特征；2) 采用基于Kalman滤波的跟踪器和运动预测模型，利用历史感知结果预测当前状态，降低传输延迟；3) 支持无需重训练即可集成未知异构智能体。

Result: CERF在保持与主流中间协作方法相当性能的同时，通信开销降低95%，并成功应用于各种下游任务。

Conclusion: CERF实现了通信高效、无需重训练的开放异构协作感知，为实际部署提供了可行方案。未来可探索更鲁棒的预测模型和更丰富的虚拟模态设计。

Abstract: Collaborative perception shares information among multiple agents to obtain a comprehensive scene representation, enhancing the perceptual capability of individual agents. However, most existing methods rely on transmitting and fusing dense feature maps for collaboration, which incurs inevitable communication overhead and heterogeneity challenges, limiting their practicality for real-world deployment. To address these challenges, we propose CERF, a novel Communication-Efficient and Retraining-Free framework for open heterogeneous collaborative perception. In CERF, we introduce a new virtual modality (termed Poture), which is generated from the perception outputs of other agents, to augment the extracted Bird's Eye View (BEV) features of the ego agent. To mitigate transmission delays, we employ a Kalman-filter based tracker and a motion forecasting model to derive the current predictions from historical perception results. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CERF achieves performance comparable to mainstream intermediate-collaboration methods while reducing communication overhead by 95% across various downstream tasks. Furthermore, CERF enables seamless integration of unknown heterogeneous agents into the existing collaborative framework without additional retraining costs. Code is available at https://github.com/uestchjw/CERF.

</details>


### [62] [ASSERT: Adaptive Stochastic Sampling for Robust Diffusion Models on Analog Compute-in-Memory Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00955)
*Yuannuo Feng,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Yuxin Xie,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在模拟计算内存(CIM)硬件上的推理噪声敏感性，发现早期去噪阶段对硬件噪声更脆弱，并提出ASSERT方法通过动态调整随机性来降低噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型图像生成质量好但推理成本高，模拟CIM可加速矩阵向量乘法，但硬件空间存储变化会扰动权重并在采样过程中累积。扩散模型对硬件噪声的时间敏感性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1)建立针对多个物理CIM芯片测量校准的噪声模型；2)分析发现早期高噪声去噪阶段比最终精炼阶段更脆弱；3)一阶轨迹分析将该行为归因于固定硬件映射导致的重复相关预测误差传播；4)提出ASSERT训练无关采样器，早期使用更高随机性并平滑过渡到确定性去噪。

Result: ASSERT在高分数据集上实现最高2.58倍低于确定性DDIM的FID，在CIFAR-10步数研究中实现7.68倍低于DDIM的FID，且不改变模型参数或网络评估次数。

Conclusion: 通过在扩散采样过程中动态调整随机性程度，可以有效降低硬件噪声对生成质量的影响，为CIM加速扩散模型推理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong image generation quality but incur high iterative denoising costs. Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) can accelerate matrix-vector multiplications, yet spatial memory variations perturb weights and accumulate during sampling. Unlike conventional neural networks, diffusion models' temporal sensitivity to hardware noise remains underexplored. We investigate diffusion inference using a noise model calibrated and validated against measurements collected from multiple physical CIM chips. Our results show that the early, high-noise denoising stage is substantially more vulnerable than the final refinement stage. A first-order trajectory analysis attributes this behavior to the repeated propagation of correlated prediction errors induced by a fixed hardware mapping. Based on this observation, we propose ASSERT, a training-free sampler that uses higher stochasticity early and smoothly transitions to deterministic denoising. The injected stochasticity changes subsequent activation trajectories and thereby reduces their alignment with persistent spatial errors. Across the evaluated settings, ASSERT achieves up to 2.58$\times$ lower FID than deterministic DDIM on high-resolution datasets and 7.68$\times$ lower FID in the CIFAR-10 step-count study, without changing model parameters or the number of network evaluations.

</details>


### [63] [PredErase: Training-Free Object-and-Effect Removal with Predictive Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00956)
*Waikit Xiu,Qiang Lu,Junbiao Chen,Xiying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PredErase是一种无需训练的推理方法，结合FLUX.2和I-JEPA模型，能够在仅使用实例掩码的情况下移除物体及其投射阴影和接触阴影。该方法通过扩展掩码暴露残留痕迹，利用I-JEPA预测的掩码区域目标结构，并通过稀疏投影梯度对齐填充结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前目标移除方法存在一个根本性问题：用户提供的实例掩码只覆盖物体本身，不包含投射阴影和接触阴影。这些光照痕迹位于掩码外部，但属于物体的"光影足迹"。现有训练-free方法将掩码视为整个可编辑区域，使用CLIP或DINO引导采样，但这些方法无法预测被遮挡的场景结构。

Method: PredErase采用以下技术路线：1）使用接触带扩展M_flux扩展原始实例掩码，暴露支持平面上的局部残留；2）利用I-JEPA的掩码token预测预训练任务，提供基于上下文的掩码区域目标表示；3）通过稀疏投影梯度将解码的Fill填充结果与I-JEPA预测的目标对齐，仅在实例内部进行编辑，外部坐标保持锁定。

Result: 在RemovalBench、RORD-Val和DEFACTO-Val数据集的仅实例掩码设置下，PredErase相比原生FLUX.2主干网络取得了提升。然而，监督学习方法在若干全图外观指标上仍表现更强。

Conclusion: PredErase实现了冻结Fill模型的无训练目标及其效果编辑，是paired-data擦除方法的补充而非替代。其核心贡献在于分离了"何处可重写像素"与"何种结构应填充空洞"两个问题，并证明了I-JEPA表示在图像修复中的潜力。

Abstract: Removing an object is not the same as filling its mask. Cast shadows and contact shading usually lie outside the user-provided instance mask M_obj, so a frozen Fill model that edits only that mask leaves the object's photometric footprint on nearby surfaces. Supervised removers learn this joint erasure from paired clean plates. Training-free editors freeze pretrained weights, yet most still treat M_obj as the entire editable support and steer sampling with CLIP or DINO energies that do not predict the occluded scene. We present PredErase, a training-free inference procedure on frozen FLUX.2 and I-JEPA. The method separates where Fill may rewrite pixels from what structure should occupy the hole. A contact-band expansion M_flux of M_obj exposes local residuals on the supporting plane. I-JEPA, pretrained for masked token prediction, supplies a context-conditioned hole target in representation space; sparse projected gradients align decoded Fill completions with that target inside the instance, while coordinates outside the packed support stay locked. Under instance-only masks on RemovalBench, RORD-Val, and DEFACTO-Val, PredErase improves the native FLUX.2 backbone. Supervised removers remain stronger on several full-image appearance metrics; the supported claim is training-free object-and-effect editing of frozen Fill, not replacement of paired-data erasers.

</details>


### [64] [Candidate-Expanding Routing with Permutation-Stabilized Experts for Mixed-Format Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00959)
*Hai-Dang Nguyen,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对混合格式医学视觉问答（VQA）中的两个挑战：选择题答案不稳定和开放文本输出格式错误。提出答案文本记忆、排列稳定的视觉-语言专家和稀疏候选扩展路由器的方案。将专家top-2作为可路由候选与记忆和专家top-1并列。在1403例回顾性分析中，二元路由从88.95%提升至91.73%（+2.78pp）， oracle覆盖率从90.31%升至96.15%，最终配置达92.23%。开放问题生成475/475个模式有效输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合格式医学VQA存在两个问题：1) 多选题预测会因选项符号或位置变化而不稳定；2) 临床合理的开放答案在序列化格式错误时无法通过自动评估。需要解决这两个挑战以提高系统稳定性和输出有效性。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：1) 答案文本记忆（answer-text memory）；2) 排列稳定的视觉-语言专家（permutation-stabilized vision-language expert）；3) 稀疏候选扩展路由器（sparse candidate-expanding router）。创新点在于将专家top-2作为可路由候选（与记忆和专家top-1并列）。使用循环调度（cyclic schedule），对开放问题采用严格生成和确定性保护。

Result: 回顾性分析（1403例）：二元路由从88.95%提升至91.73%（+2.78pp，95%CI 1.57-3.99），纠正56个错误，新增17个回归。Oracle覆盖率从90.31%升至96.15%。最终提交配置达92.23%。开放问题生成：475/475个模式有效的参与方面向输出，无需修复、重试或硬门失败。视觉消融显示显著文本依赖性。

Conclusion: 候选扩展提供了主要的受控路由增益。开放路径证据确立了输出合约有效性，但未确立临床正确性。方法有效解决了混合格式医学VQA的稳定性问题，但输出的临床正确性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Mixed-format medical visual question answering (VQA) requires stable option selection and machine-readable free-text output. The two formats fail differently: multiple-choice predictions can change with option symbols or positions, while clinically plausible open answers can fail automated evaluation when serialization is malformed. We address both challenges with an answer-text memory, a permutation-stabilized vision--language expert, and a sparse candidate- expanding router. The cyclic schedule follows prior work; our contribution is to make expert top-2 a routable candidate alongside memory and expert top-1. On a 1,403-case retrospective internal analysis, this expansion improves a matched binary router from 88.95% to 91.73% (+2.78 percentage points; 95% CI 1.57--3.99), with 56 rescued errors and 17 regressions. Oracle coverage rises from 90.31% to 96.15%, and the final submitted configuration reaches 92.23% on the same retrospective split. For open questions, strict generation and deterministic guards produce 475/475 schema- valid participant-facing outputs without repair, retry, or hard-gate failure. Visual ablations reveal substantial textual dependence. Candidate expansion supplies the principal controlled routing gain; open-path evidence establishes output-contract validity rather than clinical correctness in medical use or deployment.

</details>


### [65] [Conditional Flow Matching for Cross-Field MRI Harmonisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00960)
*Baris Imre,Aram Salehi,Levente Baljer,Andrew Webb,Marius Staring,Efe Ilicak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对跨场强脑MRI翻译的统一条件流匹配模型，能够在5种场强和3种对比度之间进行任意方向转换，仅用3个配对受试者数据训练，实现了60种场强-对比度组合的单一模型覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI图像在同一受试者不同场强下外观差异显著，这使得跨中心数据的比较和整合变得复杂，需要开发能够跨场强翻译的模型。

Method: 将问题表述为条件流匹配路径，利用源和目标体积空间配准的特性，学习将源切片直接映射到目标切片的速度场。训练采用三阶段策略：1) 退化桥预训练从大量非配对回顾性队列中提取恢复先验；2) 跨场微调在配对队列上对所有有向场强对进行微调；3) 对抗性细化增强输出锐度。推理时使用二阶Heun求解器进行速度场积分。

Result: 无配对数据学习的恢复先验已达到0.837的平均SSIM，每个后续训练阶段都有提升。最终单一6.3M参数模型覆盖全部60种场强对和对比度组合，每切片5步求解器推理。在挑战评估集上达到平均SSIM 0.909，优于基于相同网络的回归和扩散基线方法。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配方法成功解决了跨场强MRI翻译问题，三阶段训练策略有效利用了有限的配对数据和丰富的非配对数据，单一模型实现了多场强多对比度的灵活转换，性能优于传统回归和扩散方法。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance images of the same subject look markedly different across field strengths, which complicates the comparison and pooling of data across sites. We address cross-field brain-MRI translation for the MRIxFields2026 challenge, and in particular its Task~3: a single model that translates between any directed pair of the five field strengths and across three contrasts. We phrase the problem as a conditional flow matching path: because the source and target volumes are spatially registered, we learn a velocity field that carries the source slice directly to the target slice, rather than starting from noise. To learn this mapping from only three paired subjects, the unified model is trained in three stages: a degradation-bridge pretraining that distills a restoration prior from the abundant unpaired retrospective cohort, a cross-field finetuning over all directed pairs on the paired cohort, and an adversarial refinement that sharpens the output. At inference, we integrate the learned velocity with a second-order Heun solver in a handful of steps. A restoration prior learned without any paired data already reaches a mean SSIM of 0.837, and each subsequent training stage improves on it. A single 6.3M-parameter model thereby covers all 60 field-pair and contrast combinations, with inference in five solver steps per slice. On the challenge evaluation set the model reaches a mean SSIM of 0.909, averaged over the three contrasts, outperforming regression and diffusion baselines built on the identical network on all three challenge metrics.

</details>


### [66] [ReFlowSET: Representation-Aligned Latent Flow Matching for SAR-to-EO Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00968)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Seungchul Lee,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFlowSET是一个条件潜在流匹配框架，通过联合SAR-EO重建审计选择最优潜在编解码器，从零开始训练小型条件DiT进行SAR到EO图像翻译，使用冻结视觉基础模型对齐 noisy-EO 和 clean-EO 特征以提供语义引导，在QXS-SAROPT和SAR2Opt数据集上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在扩散方法继承预训练的自动编码器，但重建保真度在不同编解码器和模态间差异大，且现有方法主要使用在自然图像上预训练的编解码器，这可能不适合SAR-EO跨模态翻译任务。

Method: 1) 提出联合SAR-EO重建审计机制选择最优潜在编解码器；2) 在选定潜在空间中从零开始训练小型条件DiT；3) 使用双流SAR条件化和联合特征细化；4) 训练时将 noisy-EO 特征与冻结视觉基础模型提取的 clean-EO 表示对齐提供语义引导，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在QXS-SAROPT和SAR2Opt数据集上，ReFlowSET在多种感知保真度和分布指标上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过选择合适的编解码器、使用轻量级条件DiT和语义对齐策略，ReFlowSET成功实现了高质量的SAR到EO图像翻译，为跨模态图像翻译提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: SAR-to-EO image translation aims to generate electro-optical (EO) imagery from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations. Existing latent diffusion approaches typically inherit a predetermined autoencoder, although reconstruction fidelity can vary substantially across codecs and modalities. Because the latent codec affects the round-trip preservation of both SAR conditions and EO targets, codec selection constitutes a fundamental design choice; nevertheless, existing methods largely rely on codecs pretrained on natural images. To remedy this, we introduce ReFlowSET, a conditional latent flow-matching framework that selects its codec through a joint SAR--EO reconstruction audit. Rather than inheriting a heavyweight pretrained generator, ReFlowSET trains a substantially smaller conditional DiT from scratch in the selected latent space, using dual-stream SAR conditioning followed by joint feature refinement. To provide semantic guidance for this from-scratch training, intermediate noisy-EO features are aligned with clean target-EO representations extracted by a frozen vision foundation model. This alignment is used only during training and introduces no additional inference cost. Experiments on QXS-SAROPT and SAR2Opt demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across diverse perceptual fidelity and distributional metrics. Code and pretrained weights are publicly available at https://github.com/KAIST-VICLab/ReFlowSET.

</details>


### [67] [Semi-Supervised Virtual Staining via Morphology Preservation and Histopathological Realism Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00984)
*Baoshun Wang,Weiping Lin,Linwu Wang,Yihuang Hu,Baptiste Magnier,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种稳定的半监督虚拟染色框架，通过结合有限的成对数据和大量未成对的源图像来解决虚拟染色问题。该方法引入Hessian导出的形态保持和 histopathological 现实约束两个组件来抑制结构和外观漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟染色方法依赖严格配对和精确对齐的训练数据，而这类数据在常规实践中难以获取且成本高昂。现有方法直接纳入未配对图像时缺乏监督，容易导致不现实染色、形态退化甚至训练崩溃。

Method: 提出稳定半监督虚拟染色框架：(1) Hessian导出的形态保持从源图像中提取结构线索，约束生成输出保留组织形态；(2) Histopathological现实约束引导输出趋向可行的目标染色特征，防止结构监督退化为轮廓增强或简单颜色变换。

Result: 在H&E到Ki67和HER2的IHC翻译，以及FFPE到H&E翻译的广泛实验中，展示了图像质量、形态保持、鲁棒性和下游诊断性能的持续改进。

Conclusion: 该框架通过两个组件抑制结构和外观漂移，稳定半监督染色翻译，促进诊断相关信息保留，为实际临床应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Virtual staining aims to computationally generate target-stained histopathological images while reducing the cost and time associated with conventional staining procedures. However, existing methods rely predominantly on strictly paired and accurately registered training data, which are difficult and expensive to obtain in routine practice. To reduce this dependence, we propose a stable semi-supervised virtual staining framework that jointly exploits both limited paired data and abundant unpaired source images. Directly incorporating unpaired images is challenging because their generated results lack corresponding targets for supervision, potentially leading to unrealistic staining, morphological degradation, or even training collapse. To obtain reliable supervision from these images, Hessian-derived morphology preservation extracts structural cues from each source image and constrains the generated output to retain tissue morphology. Histopathological realism constraints further guide the output toward plausible target-stain characteristics, preventing the source-derived structural supervision from degenerating into contour enhancement or simple color transformation. Together, the two components suppress structural and appearance drift, stabilize semi-supervised stain translation, and promote the preservation of diagnostically relevant information. Extensive experiments on H&E-to-IHC translation for Ki67 and HER2, as well as FFPE-to-H&E translation, demonstrate consistent improvements in image quality, morphology preservation, robustness, and downstream diagnostic performance. Code will be available.

</details>


### [68] [EvoGS: Modeling Deformation Evolution for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00994)
*Wei Dong,Shahram Shirani,Jun Chen,Han Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoGS提出了一种基于3D高斯溅射的动态场景重建框架，将高斯变形建模为时间演化过程，通过历史状态外推和MLP校正来应对大范围或突变运动，并引入变形感知的密集化策略提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLP的3DGS动态场景方法在每个时间戳独立估计变形，对大范围或突变运动的鲁棒性较差。

Method: EvoGS为每个高斯维护持久的变形状态，从历史变形状态外推未来状态，并使用MLP导出的观测进行校正。校正通过时间残差记忆和演化统计量（变形速度、轨迹偏差）进行自适应加权。此外引入变形感知的密集化策略，沿校正后的变形方向执行克隆和分裂操作，并对变形历史不稳定的高斯使用不确定性感知策略抑制密集化。

Result: 实验表明EvoGS提升了动态新视角合成质量，在基准数据集上达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 通过将高斯变形建模为时间演化过程，EvoGS能够更好地处理动态场景中的大范围和突变运动，变形感知密集化策略进一步提升了重建质量。

Abstract: Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enable real-time novel view synthesis in dynamic scenes by learning time-conditioned Gaussian deformations. However, existing MLP-based methods typically estimate deformations independently at each timestamp, making them less robust to large or abrupt motions. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{{EvoGS}}, a 3DGS-based dynamic reconstruction framework that models Gaussian deformation as a temporal evolution process. EvoGS maintains persistent deformation states for each Gaussian, extrapolates future states from historical deformation states, and corrects the predictions with MLP-derived observations. The correction is adaptively weighted using a temporal residual memory and evolution statistics such as deformation velocity and trajectory deviation. To further improve reconstruction quality, EvoGS introduces deformation-aware densification. Clone and split operations are performed along corrected deformation directions, while an uncertainty-aware strategy suppresses densification for Gaussians with unstable deformation histories. Experiments show that EvoGS improves dynamic novel view synthesis quality and achieves competitive performance across benchmarks.

</details>


### [69] [CQF-HMR: Continuous Quaternion Flows for Probabilistic 3D Human Mesh Recovery from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00995)
*Cuong Le,Bao-Long Tran,Pavlo Melnyk,Tahereh Dehdarirad,Bastian Wandt,Mårten Wadenbäck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四元数约束的连续归一化流方法，用于从单张2D图像中概率化地恢复3D人体模型。该方法解决了3D关键点方法产生不合理姿态的问题，同时克服了SMPL方法中关节旋转非加性带来的建模复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张2D图像恢复3D人体是一个病态问题，因为深度信息丢失。现有方法存在局限性：基于3D关键点的方法往往产生难以应用于下游任务的不合理姿态；而SMPL方法虽更具可扩展性，但关节旋转的非加性特性使生成过程建模复杂。因此需要一种新的概率化方法来处理这些问题。

Method: 提出一种基于四元数约束的连续归一化流（quaternion-constrained continuous normalizing flows）方法，以2D姿态估计为条件，生成3D人体假设。该方法利用四元数表示旋转的优势，避免了其他旋转表示方法的局限性。

Result: 在Human3.6M数据集上达到最先进水平，特别是在模糊场景下表现优异；在具有挑战性的3DPW和EMDB基准测试中也获得了可比的姿态估计精度。

Conclusion: 四元数约束的连续归一化流方法为单图像3D人体恢复提供了一种有效的概率化解决方案，在姿态合理性和估计精度方面均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Recovering 3D digital humans from a single 2D image is an ill-posed computer vision problem due to the loss of depth information. Probabilistic 3D human pose estimation compensates for this by estimating a set of 3D hypotheses from a prior distribution via generative models. However, most prior work focuses only on 3D keypoints, which often leads to implausible poses that are difficult to apply to downstream tasks, e.g. animation or digital humans. SMPL-based methods are more scalable thanks to the explicit body priors, but it requires more complex modeling of the generation process due to the non-additive nature of the joint rotations. In this work, we propose a novel approach for probabilistic 3D humans using quaternion-constrained continuous normalizing flows conditioned on 2D pose estimations. Our proposed quaternion flows show significant advantages over approaches using other rotation representations. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art results of our method on Human3.6M, particularly in ambiguous settings, and comparable pose estimation accuracy on challenging 3DPW and EMDB benchmarks.

</details>


### [70] [Does This Moment Justify the Recommendation? Counterfactual Behavior-Grounded Evidence Retrieval for Personalized Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00996)
*Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究个性化视频推荐中的反事实证据检索问题，提出CBGER-10K数据集和CBGER框架，用于评估模型预测是否对证据替换保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化视频推荐和时序视频定位研究无法验证检索到的片段是否构成向特定用户推荐视频的有效证据，需要区分"在哪里"和"是否存在"个性化证据。

Method: 构建包含5000个事实-反事实对的CBGER-10K数据集；提出CBGER框架，将片段级定位与视频级证据估计解耦，通过结构化反事实监督学习。

Result: CBGER在五个基线上达到0.4432 MRR、0.6977配对准确率和0.6987干预一致性；与QD-DETR相比，MRR提升不显著但配对准确率提升11.03个百分点。

Conclusion: 准确的时序定位不一定意味着可靠的个性化证据存在，需要显式评估"是否存在"（Where）之外的"是否"（Whether）问题。

Abstract: Personalized video recommendation predicts user preference at the video level, while temporal video grounding localizes query-relevant moments. However, strong localization does not establish whether the retrieved moment constitutes valid evidence for recommending the video to a particular user. We study counterfactual behavior-grounded evidence retrieval, which separates where personalized evidence occurs from whether such evidence exists and evaluates whether model predictions respond consistently when that evidence is replaced. We introduce CBGER-10K, containing 5,000 controlled factual--counterfactual pairs for 3,026 users, where each pair replaces only the focal behavior-supported segment while preserving the user, temporal position, and hard distractors. We further propose CBGER, a compact framework that decouples segment-level localization from video-level evidence estimation and learns both through structured counterfactual supervision. CBGER achieves $0.4432$ MRR, $0.6977$ Pair Accuracy, and $0.6987$ Intervention Consistency across five adapted personalized-highlight and temporal-grounding baselines. Notably, compared with QD-DETR, its MRR improvement is not statistically significant, while Pair Accuracy improves by $11.03$ points. These results show that accurate temporal localization does not necessarily imply reliable personalized evidence existence, motivating explicit evaluation of Whether alongside Where.

</details>


### [71] [SinkPruner: Sink-Free Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01004)
*Shiyu Li,Zi-Yuan Hu,Shijia Huang,Yanyang Li,Yiwu Zhong,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SinkPruner，一种无需训练的视觉token剪枝框架，用于高效的多模态大语言模型推理。该方法通过粗到细的设计过滤高范数异常值并保留与文本语义对齐的token，在89% token减少情况下仍保持96.5%和91.8%的原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略了高范数异常值token（特征范数异常大的token），这些token在特征和空间维度上高度冗余，却被现有方法错误地保留，导致剪枝决策次优。

Method: 提出包含两个关键模块的粗到细框架：1) 视觉净化器(Visual Sanitizer)：过滤高范数冗余，缓解注意力 sink 和注意力分散；2) 文本引导剪枝器(Text-guided Pruner)：进一步保留与文本查询语义对齐的token。

Result: 在12个图像-语言和4个视频-语言基准测试中验证了有效性。LLaVA-1.5在89% token减少下保留96.5%性能，Qwen2.5-VL保留91.8%性能。视觉净化器可迁移到其他剪枝方法以提升性能。

Conclusion: SinkPruner是一种高效、有效且可泛化的视觉token剪枝框架，在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持了多模态理解能力，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Despite their strong multimodal understanding ability, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur substantial computational overhead when processing long visual token sequences. To reduce inference costs, recent studies have explored visual token pruning through vision-centric or text-guided strategies. However, these methods often overlook high-norm outlier tokens, i.e., tokens with abnormally large feature norms, leading to suboptimal pruning decisions. In this work, we show that such high-norm outlier tokens are highly redundant in both feature and spatial dimensions, yet are often mistakenly preserved as informative cues by existing methods.
  Motivated by this observation, we propose SinkPruner, a training-free visual token pruning framework for efficient MLLM inference. SinkPruner follows a coarse-to-fine design with two key modules: a visual sanitizer that filters high-norm redundancies and alleviates attention sink and attention dispersion, and a text-guided pruner that further retains tokens semantically aligned with the text query.
  Extensive experiments on twelve image-language and four video-language benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and generalizability of our framework. Notably, SinkPruner preserves 96.5% (91.8%) of the original performance of LLaVA-1.5 (Qwen2.5-VL) under an 89% token reduction. Experiments further indicate that our visual sanitizer exhibits promising transferability in enhancing the performance of existing pruning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/LaVi-Lab/SinkPruner.

</details>


### [72] [Low-Quality Face Recognition using Center Aligned Representations and Local Margin Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01014)
*Vedat Can Dilaver,Benjamin S. Riggan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低质量人脸识别（LQFR）难题，提出统一框架结合局部概率边际（LPM）、嵌套注意力模块（NAM）和质量门控协议（QGP），实现单一模型处理全质量光谱而不牺牲高质量性能，在多个基准数据集上获得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1）低质量人脸匹配困难且训练数据稀缺；2）高质量预训练模型在极低信噪比LQ图像上精度显著下降；3）在LQ数据上微调会牺牲HQ泛化能力；4）现代评估设置涉及多种质量水平的数据集，权衡问题更加突出。

Method: 提出三组件统一框架：1）LPM从模型判别性景观估计样本难度；2）NAM是低秩适配器模块，在选定transformer层中嵌入自注意力机制；3）QGP使用现成图像质量估计器在测试时调节适配器贡献，使单一模型可处理全质量光谱。

Result: 在监控场景（TinyFace、SurvFace）和标准场景（IJB-B、IJB-C）人脸识别基准上，识别和验证任务均获得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提统一框架有效解决低质量人脸识别挑战，同时保持高质量性能，在多个基准数据集上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Low-quality face recognition (LQFR) remains challenging due to the difficulty of matching degraded query (probe) images against low-quality (LQ) enrollment (gallery) imagery and the scarcity of training data for large-scale models. While recent face recognition (FR) models perform well on high-quality (HQ) imagery, their accuracy drops significantly on LQ images with extremely low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Moreover, fine-tuning HQ-pretrained models on LQ data often improves LQ recognition at the expense of HQ generalization. This trade-off becomes more pronounced in modern evaluation settings spanning multiple datasets with varying image quality levels. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework that combines three main components: (1) Local Probability Margin (LPM), which estimates per-sample difficulty directly from the model's discriminative landscape; (2) Nested Attention Module (NAM), a new low-rank adapter module that embeds a self-attention mechanism within selected transformer layers; and (3) Quality Gating Protocol (QGP), where an off-the-shelf image quality estimator modulates the adapter contribution at test time, enabling a single model to handle the full quality spectrum without sacrificing HQ performance. Experiments on surveillance (TinyFace, SurvFace) and standard (IJB-B, IJB-C) face recognition benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in both identification and verification. Code and models will be released at github.com/candllq/nam.

</details>


### [73] [Fi-ImageNet-1k: An OOD Benchmark From the Inside of the ImageNet-1k Validation Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01027)
*Ruslan Rozumnyi,Matěj Suchánek,Tomáš Vojíř,Klára Janoušková,Jiří Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fi-ImageNet-1k数据集，用于评估Out-of-Distribution (OOD)检测任务。该数据集利用ReImageNet重新标注工作，从ImageNet-1k验证集中筛选出不属于任何ImageNet-1k类别的图像，共655张图像来自522个类别，比现有OOD数据集更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准测试对分布外图像的定义或收集方式缺乏系统性，现有的ID标注数据集本身包含天然的OOD图像来源（如标注错误），作者希望利用这种标注错误构建更可靠的OOD测试集。

Method: 利用ReImageNet重新标注工作筛选出不再属于任何ImageNet-1k类别的验证图像；结合专家人工标注、MLLMs、VLMs和反向图像搜索，将每张图像与所有视觉相似的ID类别进行比较，只保留可被标注为ImageNet-1k标签空间之外具体类别的图像。

Result: Fi-ImageNet-1k包含655张图像，来自522个类别。在95%真阳性率下，假阳性率(FPR@95)最低为51%，没有一种评估的分类器与OOD检测器组合能达到更低的FPR@95。与NINCO数据集相比，该数据集对最先进的监督OOD检测方法来说难度提升3.8倍。

Conclusion: Fi-ImageNet-1k是一个具有挑战性的OOD检测基准测试集，比现有的OOD数据集（如NINCO）难度显著更高，有助于推动OOD检测领域的研究。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection predicts whether a test image belongs to none of the predefined classes. To evaluate this task, benchmarks need images from outside the in-distribution (ID) data; typically, these are defined or collected in an ad hoc fashion. Since no ground truth is perfect, ID-labeled datasets themselves contain a natural source of OOD images. We exploit such annotation errors and present Fi-ImageNet-1k, an OOD dataset built from ImageNet-1k validation images that the recent ReImageNet reannotation effort assigned to no ImageNet-1k class. Each image was examined by expert human annotators supported by evidence from MLLMs, VLMs, and reverse image search, comparing it against all visually similar ID classes. We keep only images that could be assigned a specific class outside the ImageNet-1k label space.
  The resulting Fi-ImageNet-1k, with 655 images from 522 classes, is substantially more challenging than any commonly used OOD dataset. No evaluated combination of classifier and OOD detector achieves a false positive rate below 51% at 95% true positive rate (FPR@95). Compared to the recent NINCO, our dataset is 3.8x more challenging in the FPR@95 metric for state-of-the-art supervised OOD detection methods.

</details>


### [74] [ViTAMINS: An Empirical Study of Training Self-Supervised Vision Transformers with Synthetic Hard Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01041)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Andreas Floros,Kleanthis-Marios Papadopoulos,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTAMINS是一种将合成硬负样本集成到无监督视觉Transformer预训练中以提高表示质量的方法。该方法通过简单修改现有对比框架，在ImageNet、迁移学习、图像检索、复制检测、图像和视频分割等任务上进行了全面基准测试，展现出卓越的性能和资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无监督视觉Transformer预训练方法中，对比学习可能不如生成式和自蒸馏方法。研究旨在通过引入合成硬负样本来改进对比学习框架，提升表示质量，并探索对比学习作为更简单但强大的替代方案的潜力。

Method: 将合成硬负样本集成到无监督视觉Transformer预训练中，对现有对比框架进行简单修改。

Result: ViTAMINS在多个任务上取得显著提升，表示中涌现出关于图像语义内容的显式信息，可作为优秀分类器（相比基线提升最高达+11.3%）。ViT-B超越V-JEPA（使用ViT-L），同时更具资源效率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，对比学习作为生成式和自蒸馏方法的更简单替代方案具有强大潜力，为无监督表示学习提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce ViTAMINS, a method that integrates synthetic hard negatives into unsupervised vision transformer pretraining to improve representation quality. Our approach is thoroughly benchmarked on ImageNet and transfer learning, image retrieval, copy detection, and image, video segmentation tasks. Notably, our proposed negatives give rise to emergent properties, where learned representations contain explicit information about the semantic content of an image and serve as excellent classifiers (up to +11.3% over baselines). ViTAMINS achieves these benefits through simple modifications to existing contrastive frameworks and outperforms competing methods while being more resource efficient, e.g., our ViT-B surpasses V-JEPA with ViT-L. Our findings motivate reconsidering contrastive learning as a simpler yet powerful alternative to dominant generative and self-distillation approaches.

</details>


### [75] [Dyn-3D: Unveiling and Resolving Ego-Motion Ambiguity in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01059)
*Jiayu Ding,Zhuodong Liu,Lei Zhang,Manyu Xiong,Hongbo Jin,Haoran Tang,Hongbo Zhang,Changen Zhu,Wenbo Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kinematic Collapse问题，即视觉语言模型在进行动态3D空间推理时，由于过度拟合平滑轨迹先验而非真正理解物理运动，导致在大位移情况下空间推理严重退化。为解决这一问题，作者构建了Dyn-3D基准测试集，并提出TempoVista框架及其Kinematic-GSPO算法，通过将度量物理真值嵌入策略优化来显式地将视觉表征锚定在3D空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在处理动态3D空间推理时存在根本性问题：模型往往过度拟合自然视频中的虚假视觉-运动相关性，缺乏显式的物理监督，导致在经历大位移时空间推理能力严重下降，这一现象被称为Kinematic Collapse（运动学崩溃）。

Method: 1. 构建Dyn-3D基准测试集，利用反事实3D渲染技术将视觉变化与真实运动学属性严格解耦；2. 提出TempoVista框架，包含Kinematic-GSPO算法，将度量物理真值嵌入策略优化过程；3. 利用相机动力学作为有效的几何校准信号。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著提升了运动估计能力和鲁棒的空间推理性能。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉表征显式锚定在3D空间中，利用相机动力学作为几何校准信号，可以有效解决VLM在动态3D空间推理中的Kinematic Collapse问题，为视觉语言模型的物理理解提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) tackle dynamic 3D spatial reasoning, ego-motion perception becomes essential to resolve monocular scale ambiguity. However, current models often overfit to smooth trajectory priors rather than genuinely understanding physical motion. Consequently, their spatial reasoning degrades severely under large displacements, a phenomenon we term Kinematic Collapse. This failure stems from spurious visual-motion correlations in natural videos and a lack of explicit physical supervision. To evaluate this, we introduce Dyn-3D, a benchmark using counterfactual 3D rendering to rigorously decouple visual changes from true kinematic properties. Furthermore, we propose the TempoVista framework, featuring the Kinematic-GSPO algorithm. By embedding metric physical ground truth into policy optimization, TempoVista explicitly grounds visual representations in 3D space. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves both motion estimation and robust spatial reasoning by utilizing camera dynamics as an effective geometric calibration signal.

</details>


### [76] [IT-TextFusion: Iterative Text-Image Interaction with Text-Guided Residual Refinement for Degradation-Aware Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01092)
*Siyang Liu,Peiyi Zhou,Tianle Jin,Rongrong Bian,Zheke Jin,Mengze Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代式文本引导图像融合框架，通过深层交叉注意力、多尺度交叉门融合和阶段特定文本条件调制，实现文本嵌入对分层特征融合和残差 refinement 的条件化。实验在多个基准数据集上验证了方法在信息保留和感知质量指标上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导融合方法依赖浅层语义-视觉交互和有限注意力机制，难以鲁棒处理复杂退化且未能充分利用文本引导。因此需要更深入的文本-图像特征交互机制。

Method: 提出迭代式文本引导融合框架，包含：1) 深层交叉注意力 (Deepest-level Cross-Attention)；2) 多尺度交叉门融合 (Multi-scale Cross-Gate Fusion)；3) 阶段特定文本条件调制。通过在分层解码器和 refinement 阶段重复注入池化文本嵌入，实现降解感知的全局语义条件化，同时保留可见光与红外模态的互补信息。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，本方法在信息保留和感知质量指标上有所提升，但在某些数据集上呈现指标依赖的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过文本条件的多阶段特征交互，有效提升了文本引导图像融合的性能，为多模态融合提供了新的思路，但仍存在指标权衡问题需进一步优化。

Abstract: Text-guided image fusion has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for integrating multi-modal information while enabling flexible and task-oriented fusion control. However, existing text-guided fusion methods often rely on shallow semantic-visual interaction and limited attention mechanisms, which restrict their ability to robustly handle complex degradations and fully exploit textual guidance. In this paper, we propose an iterative text-guided image fusion framework that incorporates text-conditioned feature interaction across multiple fusion and refinement stages. The proposed method integrates deepest-level Cross-Attention, multi-scale Cross-Gate Fusion, and stage-specific text-conditioned modulation, allowing the global text embedding to condition hierarchical feature fusion and residual refinement. By repeatedly injecting the pooled text embedding across hierarchical decoder and refinement stages, the proposed framework provides degradation-aware global semantic conditioning while preserving complementary information from the visible and infrared modalities. Experiments on several benchmark datasets show that the proposed method improves several information-preservation and perceptual-quality metrics, while exhibiting metric-dependent trade-offs on some datasets.

</details>


### [77] [P-PatchDiff: Progressive Patch Diffusion Models for Low-light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01123)
*Ruoyu Guo,Haonan Zhong,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P-PatchDiff，一种用于低光照图像增强的可扩展渐进式补丁扩散框架，通过在去噪过程中动态调整补丁大小，实现从局部到全局视角的逐步转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖小尺寸固定补丁（如64×64）无法捕获图像级亮度上下文；增大感受野虽能改善亮度估计但显著增加计算成本；同时低光照图像存在区域亮度不均问题，需确保局部增强的补丁在合成时保持视觉一致性。

Method: 提出P-PatchDiff框架，动态调整去噪过程中的补丁尺寸，实现从局部到全局的渐进式转变；引入多补丁对齐策略，使用估计的全局亮度代理对不同补丁尺度的特征进行归一化；注重可扩展性和整体亮度一致性而非像素级重建精度。

Result: 实验表明P-PatchDiff可有效增强400×600到4K分辨率的图像，比现有补丁扩散模型快80倍，内存占用低于9GB。

Conclusion: P-PatchDiff能够感知多尺度信息，更好地增强不同亮度的区域，为低光照图像增强提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in low-light image enhancement have leveraged diffusion models for their strong ability to generate perceptually realistic, detailed images. Patch diffusion models further offer a promising solution to size-agnostic image restoration while improving efficiency. However, existing methods typically rely on small, fixed patches (e.g., 64$\times$64) that cannot capture image-level brightness context, whereas enlarging the receptive field improves brightness and colour estimation but substantially increases computational cost. Moreover, low-light images often exhibit uneven brightness across regions, making it necessary to ensure that locally enhanced patches remain visually coherent when combined into the full image. To address these limitations, we propose P-PatchDiff, a scalable progressive patch diffusion framework for low-light image enhancement that dynamically adjusts patch size throughout the denoising process, enabling a gradual shift from local to global views. A Multi-Patch Alignment strategy is also introduced to normalise features across varying patch scales using an estimated global brightness proxy. Rather than pursuing pixel-level reconstruction accuracy, P-PatchDiff focuses on scalability and coherent brightness across the whole image, allowing the model to perceive multi-scale information and better enhance regions with varying brightness. We empirically demonstrate that P-PatchDiff effectively enhances images ranging from 400 $\times$ 600 to 4K and is 80$\times$ faster than existing patch diffusion models while using less than 9GB of memory. The code is available at https://github.com/RuoyuGuo/P-PatchDiff.

</details>


### [78] [Different Changes Require Different Reasoning: Change-Type-Specialized Experts for Robust Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01136)
*Jiyoung Park,InJae Oh,Jung Uk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEDIC是一种用于图像变化描述的新型框架，通过引入变化类型感知机制，显式建模变化类别（如颜色变化、物体添加等），利用类型专业化的记忆专家动态检索相关视觉模式，从而生成更精确的类型感知变化描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变化描述方法往往忽视不同变化类型（如颜色变化、物体添加等）具有不同的视觉线索和推理需求这一特点，导致生成描述不够精确。

Method: MEDIC框架包含类型专业化的记忆专家模块，根据输入动态检索类型相关的视觉模式。通过软路由机制将输入分配到不同的类型专家，并学习每个变化类别的专用表示，使每个专家能够捕捉其变化类型内的多样性变化同时聚焦于最关键的视觉线索。

Result: 广泛实验表明，MEDIC在多个具有挑战性的数据集上始终优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入变化类型感知机制和专业化的专家模块，MEDIC能够生成更精确、更具类型意识的变化描述，为变化描述任务提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Change captioning is the task of generating natural language descriptions that explain the changes between a pair of images. Although different change types (e.g., color shifts, object additions) exhibit distinct visual cues and require specialized reasoning processes, existing methods often overlook these distinctions. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Expert Diagnosis for Image Change (MEDIC), a novel framework that introduces change-type awareness by explicitly modeling change categories. MEDIC employs type-specialized memory experts that dynamically retrieve type-relevant visual patterns conditioned on the input. This design enables each expert to capture diverse variations within its change type while focusing on the most informative visual cues. By softly routing inputs across type-specialized experts and learning dedicated representations for each change category, MEDIC generates more precise and type-aware change descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed MEDIC consistently outperforms existing methods across diverse and challenging datasets. The code is available at \href{{https://github.com/VisualAIKHU/MEDIC}}{{GitHub}}.

</details>


### [79] [Revisiting Face Recognition for Monozygotic Twins: The Celeb Twins Test Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01141)
*Michael Zang,Haiyu Wu,Mrinal Sharma,Kevin W. Bowyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了Celeb Twins Test Set (CTTS)数据集，包含80对明星双胞胎图像，用于评估同卵双胞胎人脸识别性能。研究发现当前深度CNN模型能达到76%以上准确率，但未能有效利用皮肤标记和镜像不对称性等区分特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同卵双胞胎人脸识别是现有人脸验证系统的难点，现有人脸测试集缺乏双胞胎相关元数据，需要构建专门的数据集来研究如何区分同卵双胞胎。

Method: 构建CTTS数据集（包含皮肤标记和镜像不对称性元数据），测试多种深度CNN匹配器在CTTS上的性能，分析模型是否利用了皮肤标记和不对称性特征，并探讨生成式AI工具创建双胞胎图像的可行性。

Result: 当前深度CNN匹配器在CTTS上达到超过76%的准确率，但实验表明这些模型并未利用皮肤标记或镜像不对称性特征进行识别。

Conclusion: 现有深度学习模型在同卵双胞胎识别任务中存在特征利用不足的问题；生成式AI工具（如Grok、ChatGPT、Gemini）有望用于生成想象的双胞胎图像，以扩充训练数据集中双胞胎的代表性。

Abstract: Past literature on face recognition for monozygotic (("identical") twins points to facial marks and mirror asymmetry as possible directions for improved accuracy of twins recognition. The Celeb Twins Test Set (CTTS) contains web-scraped image pairs for 80 sets of celebrity twins. It is the only twins test set with meta-data for twins with distinguishing skin marks and possible mirror asymmetry. CTTS is organized in the manner of face verification test sets such as LFW, CALFW, CPLFW, CFP-FP, and AgeDB-30. Current deep CNN matchers can achieve over 76% accuracy in classifying CTTS same-person / different-person image pairs. We show that current matchers do not make use of skin marks, or asymmetry, and discuss reasons for this. Finally, we discuss the feasibility of using generative AI tools such as Grok, ChatGPT and Gemini to create images of imagined monozygotic twins as a means to increase representation of twins in face recognition training sets.

</details>


### [80] [StainPresetNet: Stain Preset Network for Fast Multi-to-Multi Stain Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01146)
*Hongtao Kang,Die Luo,Li Chen,Jing Cai,Junbo Hu,Xiuli Liu,Shenghua Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StainPresetNet是一种新的染色归一化框架，结合结构保持与数据集级颜色映射，同时保持计算效率。通过预设参考图像引导的像素级归一化，实现无需重新训练的多方向适应性。在细胞病理学和组织病理学数据集上的评估表明，该方法在颜色映射精度、分类器泛化能力和计算效率方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有方法的局限性：传统方法通过像素级变换提供样式灵活性但颜色映射提取不准确；现有深度学习方法虽能实现准确的数据集级颜色映射，但存在计算效率低、产生伪影、固定归一化方向需重新训练等问题。

Method: StainPresetNet结合结构保持与数据集级颜色映射，采用预设参考图像引导的像素级归一化，实现多方向适应性而无需重新训练。

Result: 在细胞病理学和组织病理学数据集上评估显示：颜色映射精度优于传统方法；有效提升诊断任务的分类器泛化能力；与现有深度学习方法相比计算开销降低90%。

Conclusion: 预设引导机制通过简单替换参考图像即可灵活调整归一化方向，克服了当前深度学习解决方案的方向刚性限制。
